Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the hypotheses framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 382. The resulting operator achieves bound O(506/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_382(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 382. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^506(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)
